관련 노트: [Celeris-WebGPU 인프라·데이터 규약](celeris-webgpu-infrastructure.md).

## 문서와 코드 차이 (11-04 대조)

비교한 판: 코드는 plynett/plynett.github.io main `ebca435`(2026-07-20)의 Celeris-WebGPU이다. 문서는 같은 커밋의 docs/·README·ARCHITECTURE.md이다. 논문은 Tavakkol & Lynett 2017(CPC), Tavakkol & Lynett 2020(CPC), Lynett et al. 2026(JWPCOE)이다.

코드의 `파일:N`은 [Celeris-WebGPU 원문](../raw/source_code/Celeris-WebGPU/) 기준이다. 이전 판은 `해시:파일:N`으로 적는다. 기존 노트 인용은 반영 전 커밋 `d8157c80` 기준이다.

| 행 ID | 항목 | 문서 원문 | 코드 원문 | 결론 | 결론 문장 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| callflow:RF-002 | adapter·입력·바인드 그룹·pipeline 순서 | `ARCHITECTURE.md:29` ` 3. Load bathymetry, optional initial condition/friction/hard-bottom grids, wave forcing, overlay imagery, and design textures. `<br>`ARCHITECTURE.md:30` ` 4. Request a WebGPU adapter/device and configure the canvas. `<br>`ARCHITECTURE.md:31` ` 5. Allocate all simulation, diagnostic, render, and readback textures. `<br>`ARCHITECTURE.md:32` ` 6. Copy CPU-loaded data into GPU textures. `<br>`ARCHITECTURE.md:33` ` 7. Fetch WGSL shader text and create compute/render pipelines. `<br>`ARCHITECTURE.md:34` ` 8. Create bind groups that match the handler and shader binding layouts. `<br>`docs/source/js/main_js.md:13` ` The initialization path loads config, bathymetry, waves, optional overlays, optional initial surfaces, and model assets. It then: `<br>`docs/source/js/main_js.md:15` `` - Requests the WebGPU adapter/device and configures the canvas. The device request asks for the adapter-supported `maxTextureDimension2D`, which allows long boundary time-series textures to exceed the default 8192 rows on GPUs that report a higher limit. ``<br>`docs/source/js/main_js.md:16` ` - Destroys old textures when a new scenario starts. `<br>`docs/source/js/main_js.md:17` ` - Creates uniform buffers for simulation, render, tridiagonal, and specialized pass constants. `<br>`docs/source/js/main_js.md:18` ` - Allocates simulation textures, diagnostics textures, sediment textures, COULWAVE textures, optional spherical metric textures, render textures, and time-series textures. `<br>`docs/source/js/main_js.md:19` ` - Copies CPU-loaded data into GPU textures. `<br>`docs/source/js/main_js.md:20` ` - Attempts Google Maps overlay loading as an optional startup step; failures reset overlay flags and do not block local/example overlay loading. `<br>`docs/source/js/main_js.md:21` ` - Fetches WGSL shader source and creates compute/render pipelines. `<br>`docs/source/js/main_js.md:22` `` - Creates all bind groups through the `Handler_*.js` modules. `` | `js/main.js:240` `    adapter = await gpu.requestAdapter(options);`<br>`js/main.js:254` `    device = await adapter.requestDevice({`<br>`js/main.js:300` `    let { bathy2D, waveData, boundaryTimeSeriesData } = await OrderedFunctions(configContent, bathymetryContent, waveContent, boundaryTimeSeriesFiles);`<br>`js/main.js:957` `    const Pass0_BindGroup = create_Pass0_BindGroup(device, Pass0_uniformBuffer, txState, txBottom, txHnear);`<br>`js/main.js:1619` `    const Pass0_Pipeline = createComputePipeline(device, Pass0_ShaderCode, Pass0_BindGroupLayout, allComputePipelines);`<br>확인한 원문: `ARCHITECTURE.md:29–34`; `docs/source/js/main_js.md:13–22`; `984091f:js/main.js:103,144,633,1212`; `js/main.js:240,254,300,957,1619`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP3RF.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP3RF.md)의 RF-002. | B 문서 오기 | 본판은 adapter/device를 얻은 뒤 OrderedFunctions를 호출한다. bind group을 만든 뒤 pipeline을 만든다. |

History textures such as `oldGradients`, `oldOldGradients`, and `predictedGradients` are shifted at the end of the timestep. `` | `js/main.js:2757–2865` 보정 블록; 2915/2924행 평균·파고; 2980행 f16; 3515행 점 시계열<br>확인한 원문: `ARCHITECTURE.md:57–59`; `984091f:js/main.js:1805–1806,1913–1919,1958–1977`; `js/main.js:2757–2865,2868–2872,2915–2931,2980,3515`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP3RF.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP3RF.md)의 RF-003. | B 문서 오기 | 평균·파고는 시간 단계마다 갱신한다. f16·점 시계열은 프레임마다 계산한다. |

| docs-architecture:CD1-341 | In NLSW mode this is effectively copied through; in Boussinesq/COULWAVE modes it is the right-hand side/solution texture around the PCR solve. | `docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:14` `` `txstateUVstar` and `current_stateUVstar` store the intermediate state used by the implicit dispersive solve. In NLSW mode this is effectively copied through; in Boussinesq/COULWAVE modes it is the right-hand side/solution texture around the PCR solve. ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:69` `` - `txtemp2_PCRx`, `txtemp2_PCRy`: solution textures written only by the final PCR iteration of each directional solve. `` | `shaders/Pass3_Bous.wgsl:426` `     textureStore(txNewState, idx, newState); `<br>`shaders/Pass3_Bous.wgsl:427` `     textureStore(dU_by_dt, idx, d_by_dt); `<br>`shaders/Pass3_Bous.wgsl:428` `     textureStore(F_G_star, idx, F_G_here); `<br>`shaders/Pass3_Bous.wgsl:429` `     textureStore(current_stateUVstar, idx, newState); `<br>`js/Run_Tridiag_Solver.js:66` `         runCopyTextures(device, calc_constants, txtemp2_PCRx, txNewState) `<br>`js/Run_Tridiag_Solver.js:91` `         runCopyTextures(device, calc_constants, txtemp2_PCRy, txNewState) `<br>`js/main.js:2900` `                 runCopyTextures_EncStack(commandEncoderStack, calc_constants, txNewState, txState) `<br>`js/main.js:2901` `                 runCopyTextures_EncStack(commandEncoderStack, calc_constants, current_stateUVstar, txstateUVstar) `<br>확인한 원문: `docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:14,69`; `shaders/Pass3_Bous.wgsl:426–429`; `js/Run_Tridiag_Solver.js:37–38,66,91`; `js/main.js:2900–2901`; `shaders/TriDiag_PCRx.wgsl:70–72,101–104`; `shaders/TriDiag_PCRy.wgsl:70–72,101–104`; `shaders/TriDiag_PCRx_COULWAVE.wgsl:72–74,101–107`; `shaders/Update_TriDiag_coef_COULWAVE.wgsl:51–52,84–85,114–115`; `984091f:docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:14,69`; `984091f:js/Run_Tridiag_Solver.js:37–38,66,91`; `d41fbd8:js/Run_Tridiag_Solver.js:37–38,66,91`.<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1b.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1b.md)의 CD1-341. | B 문서 오기 | `if (calc_constants.NLSW_or_Bous == 0)`이면 `runCopyTextures(device, calc_constants, current_stateUVstar, txNewState)`를 실행한다. Boussinesq 경로는 `current_stateUVstar`에서 PCR의 초기 우변을 읽는다. COULWAVE 경로는 중간 상태에서 만든 계수 텍스처의 a 채널에서 PCR의 초기 우변을 읽는다. 각 방향의 PCR 해는 `txtemp2_PCRx` 또는 `txtemp2_PCRy`에 기록한다. 호스트는 그 해를 `txNewState`로 복사한다. 문서에서 UVstar를 PCR의 solution texture라고 부른 부분은 오기다. |

| docs-architecture:CD1-872 | nestedGridOutput_dt의 파생식 | `docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:55` ` Nested-grid boundary output: `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:57` `` - `nestedGridOutput_i0`, `nestedGridOutput_j0`, `nestedGridOutput_i1`, `nestedGridOutput_j1` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:58` `` - `nestedGridOutput_start_time`, `nestedGridOutput_end_time`, `nestedGridOutput_dt` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:59` `` - `nestedGridOutput_max_samples`, `nestedGridOutput_sample_count`, `nestedGridOutput_sample_index` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:60` `` - `nestedGridOutput_trigger`, `nestedGridOutput_active` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:61` `` - `nestedGridOutput_rectangles` for optional multi-rectangle output specs ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:63` `` The scalar settings define the legacy rectangle whose four edges are sampled as `eta/hu/hv` output files compatible with boundary type `5`. Existing configs that only use these scalar fields remain valid. When `nestedGridOutput_rectangles` contains enabled entries, each entry can define `i0`, `j0`, `i1`, `j1`, `start_time`, `end_time`, `dt`, `eta_threshold`, and `file_prefix`/`name`. If a rectangle omits `file_prefix`, the default is `nestedGridOutput_file_prefix` for the first rectangle, then that base plus numeric suffixes for later rectangles, for example `gridD`, `gridD2`, `gridD3`. Each rectangle's sample count is capped at `8192` by increasing its output `dt` and logging a warning. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:87` ` ## Derived Values `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:89` `` `init_sim_parameters()` computes values that are not meant to be edited directly: ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:91` `` - `dt` from grid spacing, gravity, base depth, and Courant-style settings. Each rectangle's sample count is capped at `8192` by increasing its output `dt` and logging a warning. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:87` ` ## Derived Values `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:89` `` `init_sim_parameters()` computes values that are not meant to be edited directly: ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:91` `` - `dt` from grid spacing, gravity, base depth, and Courant-style settings. For `grid_type == 2`, `dx` is `dlon` in degrees, `dy` is `dlat` in degrees, and the timestep uses the minimum spherical physical spacing across the latitude range. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:92` `` - `DispatchX`/`DispatchY` from grid size and workgroup size. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:93` `` - `Px`/`Py`, the PCR iteration counts. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:94` `` - Inverse grid spacing and powers such as `one_over_d2x`, `one_over_d3x`, and `one_over_dxdy`. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:95` ` - Boundary reflection/shift indices. `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:96` ` - Render/canvas ratios and colorbar coordinates. `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:97` ` - Sediment settling/erosion coefficients from configured material properties. `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:99` ` When a scenario behaves oddly, check whether the value is directly loaded from JSON or derived after loading. The latter may be overwritten every time UI changes trigger recalculation. `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:101` `` For `grid_type == 2`, spherical-coordinate support is currently NLSW-only. The setup path forces Boussinesq/COULWAVE, high-order reconstruction, sediment transport, and the Cartesian breaking model off for that mode. `` | `js/constants_load_calc.js:595` `         calc_constants.nestedGridOutput_rectangle_count = calc_constants.nestedGridOutput_rectangles.filter((rectangle) => Number(rectangle?.enabled ?? 1) != 0).length; `<br>확인한 원문: `docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:55–63,87–101`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:594–598`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1e.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1e.md)의 CD1-875. | C 문서 누락 | nestedGridOutput_rectangle_count의 계산 원문은 다음과 같다.<br>``if (Array.isArray(calc_constants.nestedGridOutput_rectangles) && calc_constants.nestedGridOutput_rectangles.length > 0) {``<br>``calc_constants.nestedGridOutput_rectangle_count = calc_constants.nestedGridOutput_rectangles.filter((rectangle) => Number(rectangle?.enabled ?? 1) != 0).length;``<br>``} else {``<br>``calc_constants.nestedGridOutput_rectangle_count = 1;``<br>``}`` |

| docs-architecture:CD1-906 | 설정의 재실행 병합 | `docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:5` ` ## Loading Order `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:7` ` The typical loading flow is: `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:9` `` 1. `calc_constants` starts with defaults in `constants_load_calc.js`. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:10` `` 2. `loadConfig()` fetches the selected example `config.json`, if one is selected. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:11` `` 3. `init_sim_parameters()` merges uploaded or example config values onto the defaults. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:12` ` 4. Derived values are recalculated: dispatch counts, timestep, Boussinesq coefficients, inverse grid spacing, boundary reflection indices, canvas/render dimensions, sediment coefficients, and colorbar layout. `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:13` ` 5. Uniform buffers are updated from the resulting values. `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:15` ` There is no schema validator. Unknown keys may be ignored, and missing keys fall back to default JavaScript values if the code path reads them. `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:41` ` Boundary and forcing: `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:43` `` - `west_boundary_type`, `east_boundary_type`, `south_boundary_type`, `north_boundary_type` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:44` `` - `BoundaryWidth` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:45` `` - `incident_wave_type` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:46` `` - `incident_wave_H`, `incident_wave_T`, `incident_wave_direction` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:47` `` - `numberOfWaves` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:48` `` - `ts_west_file`, `ts_east_file`, `ts_south_file`, `ts_north_file` for boundary type `5` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:49` ` - river stage/discharge fields for flood scenarios `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:51` `` For `incident_wave_type == 0`, `main.js` treats the UI values as a single sine-wave component, converts height to amplitude with `H / 2`, converts direction from degrees to radians, sets `numberOfWaves` to 1, and reuploads `txWaves`. For `incident_wave_type == 1`, `Wave_Generator.js` builds a cached TMA directional spectrum from the same height, period, and direction controls, sets `numberOfWaves` to the generated component count, and reuploads `txWaves`. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:53` `` Boundary type `5` reads `eta`, `hu`, and `hv` from side-specific text files. In custom local runs, the Start Here panel can upload west/east/south/north boundary time-series files directly; uploaded files override the matching `ts_*_file` path. All active type-5 boundary files must use the same time vector. Station locations are interpreted as model x/y coordinates for Cartesian grids and absolute longitude/latitude degrees for `grid_type == 2`. `` | `js/constants_load_calc.js:438` `         var configJson = JSON.parse(configContent); `<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:439` `  `<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:440` `         // Here, you can handle the configJson object similarly to how you did in your loadConfig function. `<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:441` `         // Assuming loadedConfig and calc_constants are accessible in this scope. `<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:442` `         loadedConfig = configJson; In custom local runs, the Start Here panel can upload west/east/south/north boundary time-series files directly; uploaded files override the matching `ts_*_file` path. All active type-5 boundary files must use the same time vector. | docs-architecture:CD1-910 | 마찰 파일 선택의 실제 조건 | `docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:5` ` ## Loading Order `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:7` ` The typical loading flow is: `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:9` `` 1. `calc_constants` starts with defaults in `constants_load_calc.js`. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:10` `` 2. `loadConfig()` fetches the selected example `config.json`, if one is selected. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:11` `` 3. `init_sim_parameters()` merges uploaded or example config values onto the defaults. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:12` ` 4. Derived values are recalculated: dispatch counts, timestep, Boussinesq coefficients, inverse grid spacing, boundary reflection indices, canvas/render dimensions, sediment coefficients, and colorbar layout. `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:13` ` 5. Uniform buffers are updated from the resulting values. `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:15` ` There is no schema validator. Unknown keys may be ignored, and missing keys fall back to default JavaScript values if the code path reads them. `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:41` ` Boundary and forcing: `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:43` `` - `west_boundary_type`, `east_boundary_type`, `south_boundary_type`, `north_boundary_type` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:44` `` - `BoundaryWidth` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:45` `` - `incident_wave_type` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:46` `` - `incident_wave_H`, `incident_wave_T`, `incident_wave_direction` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:47` `` - `numberOfWaves` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:48` `` - `ts_west_file`, `ts_east_file`, `ts_south_file`, `ts_north_file` for boundary type `5` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:49` ` - river stage/discharge fields for flood scenarios `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:51` `` For `incident_wave_type == 0`, `main.js` treats the UI values as a single sine-wave component, converts height to amplitude with `H / 2`, converts direction from degrees to radians, sets `numberOfWaves` to 1, and reuploads `txWaves`. For `incident_wave_type == 1`, `Wave_Generator.js` builds a cached TMA directional spectrum from the same height, period, and direction controls, sets `numberOfWaves` to the generated component count, and reuploads `txWaves`. ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:53` `` Boundary type `5` reads `eta`, `hu`, and `hv` from side-specific text files. In custom local runs, the Start Here panel can upload west/east/south/north boundary time-series files directly; uploaded files override the matching `ts_*_file` path. All active type-5 boundary files must use the same time vector. Station locations are interpreted as model x/y coordinates for Cartesian grids and absolute longitude/latitude degrees for `grid_type == 2`. `` | `js/main.js:623` `     if(frictionFile){ `<br>`js/main.js:624` `         console.log('Loading Friction Map File') `<br>`js/main.js:625` `         calc_constants.loadFriction = 1; `<br>`js/main.js:626` `         const frictionContent = await frictionFile.text(); `<br>`js/main.js:627` `         let friction2D = await loadFrictionSurface(frictionContent, calc_constants);  `<br>`js/main.js:628` `         writeStateFlag = 1; // writing channel 1 `<br>`js/main.js:629` `         copyInitialConditionDataToTexture(calc_constants, device, friction2D, txBottomFriction, writeStateFlag) `<br>`js/main.js:630` `     } else  {     `<br>`js/main.js:631` `         copyConstantValueToTexture(calc_constants, device, txBottomFriction, calc_constants.friction, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0); `<br>`js/main.js:632` `     } `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:146` ` export async function loadFrictionSurface(frictionContent, calc_constants) { `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:147` `     let response; `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:148` `     let lines; `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:149` `     let filePath; `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:150` `     // Try to parse the uploaded content, if fails, then load server side file `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:151` `     try { `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:152` `  `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:153` `         lines = frictionContent.split('\n'); `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:154` `  `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:155` `         console.log("Friction data loaded successfully from the uploaded file."); `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:156` `  `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:157` `     } catch (error) { `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:158` `         console.log("Loading server side example friction file"); `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:159` `         filePath = calc_constants.exampleDirs[calc_constants.run_example] + 'friction.txt'; `<br>확인한 원문: `js/main.js:623–632`<br>`js/File_Loader.js:146–159`<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:5–15,41–53`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1f.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1f.md)의 CD1-910. | C 문서 누락 | 초기화 호스트는 frictionFile이 있을 때만 마찰 파일 로더를 호출한다. loadFriction == 1만 지정하면 예제 friction.txt를 자동 로드하는 경로로 들어가지 않는다. |

| docs-architecture:CD1-917 | 경계 시계열 주석 제거 | `docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:53` `` Boundary type `5` reads `eta`, `hu`, and `hv` from side-specific text files. In custom local runs, the Start Here panel can upload west/east/south/north boundary time-series files directly; uploaded files override the matching `ts_*_file` path. All active type-5 boundary files must use the same time vector. Station locations are interpreted as model x/y coordinates for Cartesian grids and absolute longitude/latitude degrees for `grid_type == 2`. ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:117` ` ## Boundary Time-Series Forcing Textures `<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:119` `` Boundary type `5` uses four optional `rgba32float` textures: `txBoundaryTimeSeriesSouth`, `txBoundaryTimeSeriesNorth`, `txBoundaryTimeSeriesWest`, and `txBoundaryTimeSeriesEast`. ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:121` `` Each active side texture stores station locations in row `0`, one time row per supplied time in rows `1..N`, and an appended final zero row used after the supplied series ends. Data rows store `[eta, hu, hv, 0]` per station. JavaScript uploads one shared time bracket to `BoundaryPass`; the shader handles interpolation along the boundary coordinate. `` | `js/File_Loader.js:317` ` function cleanBoundaryTimeSeriesLines(fileContents) { `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:318` `     return fileContents `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:319` `         .split(/\r?\n/) `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:320` `         .map(line => line.replace(/\/\/.*$/g, "").replace(/%.*$/g, "").replace(/#.*$/g, "").trim()) `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:321` `         .filter(line => line.length > 0); `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:322` ` } `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:323` `  `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:324` ` function parseBoundaryTimeSeriesNumericLine(line, filePath, lineNumber) { `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:325` `     const values = line.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).map(Number); `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:326` `     if (values.some(value => !Number.isFinite(value))) { `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:327` ``         throw new Error(`Could not parse numeric values in ${filePath} at data line ${lineNumber}.`); ``<br>`js/File_Loader.js:328` `     } `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:329` `     return values; `<br>확인한 원문: `js/File_Loader.js:317–329`<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:53`<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:117–121`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1f.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1f.md)의 CD1-917. | C 문서 누락 | 로더는 //,% ,# 뒤를 주석으로 제거한다. 수치 토큰이 유한하지 않으면 오류를 던진다. |

In custom local runs, the Start Here panel can upload west/east/south/north boundary time-series files directly; uploaded files override the matching `ts_*_file` path. All active type-5 boundary files must use the same time vector. Station locations are interpreted as model x/y coordinates for Cartesian grids and absolute longitude/latitude degrees for `grid_type == 2`. ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:117` ` ## Boundary Time-Series Forcing Textures `<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:119` `` Boundary type `5` uses four optional `rgba32float` textures: `txBoundaryTimeSeriesSouth`, `txBoundaryTimeSeriesNorth`, `txBoundaryTimeSeriesWest`, and `txBoundaryTimeSeriesEast`. ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:121` `` Each active side texture stores station locations in row `0`, one time row per supplied time in rows `1..N`, and an appended final zero row used after the supplied series ends. Data rows store `[eta, hu, hv, 0]` per station. JavaScript uploads one shared time bracket to `BoundaryPass`; the shader handles interpolation along the boundary coordinate. `` | `js/File_Loader.js:433` ` function validateSharedBoundaryTimeVector(sharedTimes, candidateTimes, filePath) { `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:434` `     if (sharedTimes.length != candidateTimes.length) { `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:435` ``         throw new Error(`Boundary time-series file ${filePath} does not match the shared time-vector length.`); ``<br>`js/File_Loader.js:436` `     } `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:437` `     for (let timeIndex = 0; timeIndex < sharedTimes.length; timeIndex += 1) { `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:438` `         const tolerance = Math.max(1.0e-7, 1.0e-7 * Math.abs(sharedTimes[timeIndex])); `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:439` `         if (Math.abs(sharedTimes[timeIndex] - candidateTimes[timeIndex]) > tolerance) { `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:440` ``             throw new Error(`Boundary time-series file ${filePath} does not share the same time vector at row ${timeIndex + 1}.`); ``<br>`js/File_Loader.js:441` `         } `<br>확인한 원문: `js/File_Loader.js:433–441`<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:53`<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:117–121`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1f.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1f.md)의 CD1-920. | C 문서 누락 | `if (sharedTimes.length != candidateTimes.length)`이면 오류를 던진다. 허용오차는 `const tolerance = Math.max(1.0e-7, 1.0e-7 * Math.abs(sharedTimes[timeIndex]));`이다. `if (Math.abs(sharedTimes[timeIndex] - candidateTimes[timeIndex]) > tolerance)`이면 오류를 던진다. |
| docs-architecture:CD1-953 | 중첩 출력 인덱스·장치 한계 검사 | `docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:55` ` Nested-grid boundary output: `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:57` `` - `nestedGridOutput_i0`, `nestedGridOutput_j0`, `nestedGridOutput_i1`, `nestedGridOutput_j1` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:58` `` - `nestedGridOutput_start_time`, `nestedGridOutput_end_time`, `nestedGridOutput_dt` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:59` `` - `nestedGridOutput_max_samples`, `nestedGridOutput_sample_count`, `nestedGridOutput_sample_index` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:60` `` - `nestedGridOutput_trigger`, `nestedGridOutput_active` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:61` `` - `nestedGridOutput_rectangles` for optional multi-rectangle output specs ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:63` `` The scalar settings define the legacy rectangle whose four edges are sampled as `eta/hu/hv` output files compatible with boundary type `5`. Existing configs that only use these scalar fields remain valid. When `nestedGridOutput_rectangles` contains enabled entries, each entry can define `i0`, `j0`, `i1`, `j1`, `start_time`, `end_time`, `dt`, `eta_threshold`, and `file_prefix`/`name`. If a rectangle omits `file_prefix`, the default is `nestedGridOutput_file_prefix` for the first rectangle, then that base plus numeric suffixes for later rectangles, for example `gridD`, `gridD2`, `gridD3`. Each rectangle's sample count is capped at `8192` by increasing its output `dt` and logging a warning. ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:123` ` ## Nested-Grid Boundary Output Textures `<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:125` `` Nested-grid boundary output dynamically allocates four temporary `rgba32float` textures per active output rectangle when capture starts: ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:127` ` - South and north output textures have width equal to the rectangle x-edge station count and height equal to the capped number of output samples. `<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:128` ` - West and east output textures have width equal to the rectangle y-edge station count and height equal to the capped number of output samples. `<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:129` `` - Each pixel stores `[eta, hu, hv, 0]`. ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:131` `` `ExtractNestedBoundaryTimeSeries.wgsl` writes these textures only at requested output times. Multi-rectangle output reuses the same shader and texture layout once per active rectangle, with independent uniform buffers, sample indices, thresholds, prefixes, and deferred readbacks. JavaScript records the actual sampled time values separately, waits until no rectangles are still actively sampling, then reads each completed rectangle's textures once and writes text files compatible with boundary type `5`. `` | `js/main.js:2097` `         for (const spec of rectangleSpecs) { `<br>`js/main.js:2098` `             const iLower = clampNestedGridOutputIndex(spec.i0, calc_constants.WIDTH - 1); `<br>확인한 원문: `js/main.js:2097–2128`<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:55–63`<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:123–131`<br>`js/main.js:1927–1928`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1f.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1f.md)의 CD1-953. | C 문서 누락 | 인덱스는 `return Math.max(0, Math.min(maxIndex, Math.round(Number(value) \|\| 0)));`으로 반올림·제한한다. 두 끝 인덱스는 최소값과 최대값으로 정렬한다. `if (sampleInfo.startTime < currentTime)`이면 시작시각을 현재시각으로 옮긴다. 그 안의 `if (sampleInfo.endTime < currentTime)`이면 종료시각도 현재시각으로 옮긴다. `if (nx > maxTextureDimension2D \|\| ny > maxTextureDimension2D \|\| sampleInfo.sampleCount > maxTextureDimension2D)`이면 해당 사각형을 건너뛴다. |

| docs-architecture:CD1-957 | 중첩 출력 텍스트 순서 | `docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:55` ` Nested-grid boundary output: `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:57` `` - `nestedGridOutput_i0`, `nestedGridOutput_j0`, `nestedGridOutput_i1`, `nestedGridOutput_j1` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:58` `` - `nestedGridOutput_start_time`, `nestedGridOutput_end_time`, `nestedGridOutput_dt` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:59` `` - `nestedGridOutput_max_samples`, `nestedGridOutput_sample_count`, `nestedGridOutput_sample_index` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:60` `` - `nestedGridOutput_trigger`, `nestedGridOutput_active` ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:61` `` - `nestedGridOutput_rectangles` for optional multi-rectangle output specs ``<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:63` `` The scalar settings define the legacy rectangle whose four edges are sampled as `eta/hu/hv` output files compatible with boundary type `5`. Existing configs that only use these scalar fields remain valid. When `nestedGridOutput_rectangles` contains enabled entries, each entry can define `i0`, `j0`, `i1`, `j1`, `start_time`, `end_time`, `dt`, `eta_threshold`, and `file_prefix`/`name`. If a rectangle omits `file_prefix`, the default is `nestedGridOutput_file_prefix` for the first rectangle, then that base plus numeric suffixes for later rectangles, for example `gridD`, `gridD2`, `gridD3`. Each rectangle's sample count is capped at `8192` by increasing its output `dt` and logging a warning. ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:123` ` ## Nested-Grid Boundary Output Textures `<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:125` `` Nested-grid boundary output dynamically allocates four temporary `rgba32float` textures per active output rectangle when capture starts: ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:127` ` - South and north output textures have width equal to the rectangle x-edge station count and height equal to the capped number of output samples. `<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:128` ` - West and east output textures have width equal to the rectangle y-edge station count and height equal to the capped number of output samples. `<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:129` `` - Each pixel stores `[eta, hu, hv, 0]`. ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:131` `` `ExtractNestedBoundaryTimeSeries.wgsl` writes these textures only at requested output times. Multi-rectangle output reuses the same shader and texture layout once per active rectangle, with independent uniform buffers, sample indices, thresholds, prefixes, and deferred readbacks. JavaScript records the actual sampled time values separately, waits until no rectangles are still actively sampling, then reads each completed rectangle's textures once and writes text files compatible with boundary type `5`. `` | `js/File_Writer.js:726` ` function createNestedGridBoundaryTimeSeriesBlob(sideData, textureData, capturedTimes, startSampleIndex = 0) { `<br>`js/File_Writer.js:727` `     const pieces = []; | docs-architecture:CD1-958 | 일반·중첩 점 시계열의 시간 | `docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:113` ` ## Time Series Textures `<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:115` `` `txTimeSeries_Locations` stores selected grid coordinates. `ExtractTimeSeries.wgsl` writes sampled values into `txTimeSeries_Data`, where element 0 is reserved for tooltip data and elements 1..N are time-series probes. ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:117` ` ## Boundary Time-Series Forcing Textures `<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:119` `` Boundary type `5` uses four optional `rgba32float` textures: `txBoundaryTimeSeriesSouth`, `txBoundaryTimeSeriesNorth`, `txBoundaryTimeSeriesWest`, and `txBoundaryTimeSeriesEast`. ``<br>`docs/architecture/DATA_AND_TEXTURES.md:121` `` Each active side texture stores station locations in row `0`, one time row per supplied time in rows `1..N`, and an appended final zero row used after the supplied series ends. Data rows store `[eta, hu, hv, 0]` per station. JavaScript uploads one shared time bracket to `BoundaryPass`; the shader handles interpolation along the boundary coordinate. `` | `js/main.js:3475` `         const pointTimeSeriesResetRequested = calc_constants.updateTimeSeriesTx == 1 \|\| `<br>`js/main.js:3476` `             (calc_constants.chartDataUpdate == 1 && chartDataUpdateResetHandled == 0); Click **Start Simulation**.   `<br>`docs/architecture/CONFIGURATION.md:53` `` Boundary type `5` reads `eta`, `hu`, and `hv` from side-specific text files. In custom local runs, the Start Here panel can upload west/east/south/north boundary time-series files directly; uploaded files override the matching `ts_*_file` path. All active type-5 boundary files must use the same time vector. | docs-architecture:CD1-1089 | designcomponent_Radius-input / value=없음 | `docs/source/js/main_js.md:102` `` - Linear-structure management for the engineered-design panel. Crest elevation, crest width, side slope, current endpoint selection, and start/end coordinates are stored in `calc_constants`; right-clicking in Design mode while the engineered-design panel is open records the selected endpoint. The preview is plotted only while that panel is open. The Add Linear Structure button validates the endpoints and queues a `MouseClickChange.wgsl` bathy/topo edit; after the GPU copy-back and near-dry/tridiagonal refresh complete, the stored endpoints and preview are reset. `` | `index.html:669` `                     <input type="number" id="designcomponent_xLoc-input" name="designcomponent_xLocValue"> `<br>`index_nots.html:629` `                     <input type="number" id="designcomponent_xLoc-input" name="designcomponent_xLocValue"> `<br>`river.html:593` `                         <input type="number" id="designcomponent_xLoc-input" name="designcomponent_xLocValue"> `<br>`MC_examples.html:602` `                     <input type="number" id="designcomponent_xLoc-input" name="designcomponent_xLocValue"> `<br>`transect_version/index.html:599` `                     <input type="number" id="designcomponent_xLoc-input" name="designcomponent_xLocValue"> `<br>확인한 원문: `index.html:669`; `index_nots.html:629`; `river.html:593`; `MC_examples.html:602`; `transect_version/index.html:599`; `js/main.js:4715`; `js/main.js:5143–5157`; `docs/source/js/main_js.md:102`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1g.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CD1g.md)의 CD1-1095. | C 문서 누락 | designcomponent_xLoc-input의 HTML value 속성은 없음이다. |

RMS 파고는 `let H_mean = sigma * 2.829;`다. 유의파고는 `let H_sig = sigma * 4.000;`다. g채널은 `(H_sig-old_vals.b)/old_vals.b`다. 이 식에는 이전 H_sig가 0인 경우의 분모 보호가 없다. [11-04 전수 검증 정정] |

| docs-source:CD2-429 | `docs/source/js/File_Loader_js.md:25` | `docs/source/js/File_Loader_js.md:25` `` `loadBoundaryTimeSeriesData()` loads active boundary type `5` files named by `ts_west_file`, `ts_east_file`, `ts_south_file`, and related side keys. User-uploaded boundary time-series files from the Start Here panel override the matching config path, which allows files outside the served example tree to be used. The parser accepts a station count, station locations, then rows of `time eta1 hu1 hv1 ...`; all active boundary files must share the same time vector. The first shared time value is copied into `calc_constants.start_time_shift`, so nested child simulations use the parent/global time clock directly. `` | `js/File_Loader.js:350` ` function parseBoundaryTimeSeriesText(fileContents, side, filePath) { `<br>`js/File_Loader.js:351` `     const lines = cleanBoundaryTimeSeriesLines(fileContents); | docs-source:CD2-430 | `docs/source/js/File_Loader_js.md:11` | `docs/source/js/File_Loader_js.md:11` `` The bathymetry and surface loaders read whitespace-delimited text grids sized by `WIDTH x HEIGHT`. `loadDepthSurface()` fetches the example `bathy.txt` unless a user file is supplied. | docs-source:CD2-452 | `docs/source/js/File_Writer_js.md:14` | `docs/source/js/File_Writer_js.md:14` `` - `writeNestedGridBoundaryTimeSeriesData()`: reads one rectangle state's four deferred nested-grid boundary output textures once at the end of capture and writes boundary type `5` compatible text files. When the rectangle-specific threshold, or fallback `nestedEtaWriteThreshold`, is greater than zero, it trims leading samples until eta magnitude on at least one rectangle-edge point exceeds the threshold. | docs-source:CD2-453 | `docs/source/js/agent_controls_js.md:126` | `docs/source/js/agent_controls_js.md:126` `` Time-series commands expose the existing root CELERIS gauge controls. Count commands update `NumberOfTimeSeries` and request a chart rebuild. Duration commands update only `maxdurationTimeSeries`, matching the native duration input path; they should not set `chartDataUpdate` or reset the active gauge extraction window. Location commands update `changethisTimeSeries` and `locationOfTimeSeries`. `timeseries.prepare_click_location` switches the runner to Design mode, marks the time-series panel active, and sets the Agent design interaction mode to `timeseries`, so right-click placement updates the selected gauge without activating linear-structure or surface-cover workflows. `` | `js/agent_controls.js:325` `     function normalizeTimeSeriesIndex(value, fallback = 1) { `<br>`js/agent_controls.js:326` `         const numeric = Number(value); `<br>`js/agent_controls.js:327` `         const base = Number.isFinite(numeric) ? | tavakkol2020:CP2-140 | 시간 변동 수심과 교란 입력 | Tavakkol 2020 p.6은 고립파 초기조건을 예로 든다. 동적 바닥 forcing은 설명하지 않는다. | `shaders/Pass3_Bous.wgsl:306` `let dhdt = textureLoad(txBoundaryForcing, idx, 0).y;`; `js/constants_load_calc.js:147` `disturbance_change_timescale: 0.0,  // these are for the depth change landslide option`<br>확인한 원문: Tavakkol 2020 p.6; `shaders/Pass3_Bous.wgsl:306,392`; `js/constants_load_calc.js:137–155`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md)의 CP2-140. | C 문서 누락 | 교란에는 위치·진폭·방향·폭·길이·dip·rake·변동 시간·궤적 설정이 있다(`js/constants_load_calc.js:137–155`). 이 설정을 2020 CML의 인쇄된 옵션 이름으로 쓰지 않는다. |
| tavakkol2020:CP2-145 | 평균·최댓값·통계 파고 | Tavakkol 2020 p.9는 수위 실시간 곡선을 설명한다. 이 통계식을 적지 않는다. | `shaders/CalcMeans.wgsl:78` `let means_new = means_here*old_frac + state_here*update_frac;`; `shaders/CalcWaveHeight.wgsl:31` `let eta = eta_New-eta_mean;`<br>`shaders/CalcWaveHeight.wgsl:33` `sum_eta2 = sum_eta2 + eta * eta;`<br>`shaders/CalcWaveHeight.wgsl:35` `var variance = sum_eta2 / f32(globals.n_time_steps_waveheight);`<br>`shaders/CalcWaveHeight.wgsl:37` `let sigma = sqrt(variance);`<br>`shaders/CalcWaveHeight.wgsl:39` `let H_mean = sigma * 2.829;  // RMS wave height`<br>`shaders/CalcWaveHeight.wgsl:40` `let H_sig = sigma * 4.000;   // sig wave height`<br>`shaders/CalcWaveHeight.wgsl:42` `let new_vals = vec4<f32>(sum_eta2, (H_sig-old_vals.b)/old_vals.b, H_sig, H_mean);`<br>확인한 원문: Tavakkol 2020 p.9; `shaders/CalcMeans.wgsl:75–78`; `shaders/CalcWaveHeight.wgsl:21–42`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md)의 CP2-145. | C 문서 누락 | 현재 코드는 각 호출에서 `let eta = eta_New-eta_mean;`을 계산한다. 이어서 `sum_eta2 = sum_eta2 + eta * eta;`를 누적한다. `var variance = sum_eta2 / f32(globals.n_time_steps_waveheight);`로 분산을 계산한다. RMS 파고는 `sigma * 2.829`이다. 유의파고는 `sigma * 4.000`이다. [11-04 전수 검증 보완] |
| tavakkol2020:CP2-146 | 면 출력 자료 구조 | Tavakkol 2020 p.9는 출력의 Advent 형식 승계만 적는다. | `js/File_Writer.js:8` `const requiredBytesPerRow = Math.ceil((width * 4 * 4) / 256) * 256; // width * RGBA * f32, padded`; `js/File_Writer.js:29` `flatData[rowBaseReal + x] = bufferData[rowBasePadded + (x << 2) + chanOffset];`<br>확인한 원문: Tavakkol 2020 p.9; `js/File_Writer.js:4–34`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md)의 CP2-146. | C 문서 누락 | GPU readback 행 크기는 `Math.ceil((width * 4 * 4) / 256) * 256`이다. 출력은 정렬용 padding을 제외한 `Float32Array(width * height)`이다. x index가 먼저 변한다. |
| tavakkol2020:CP2-148 | 시계열 export와 비동기 초기화 검사 | Tavakkol 2020 p.9는 부이의 화면 수위 곡선을 설명한다. | `js/Time_Series.js:108` `if (timeSeriesDataEpoch != calc_constants.timeSeriesDataEpoch) {`<br>`js/Time_Series.js:109` `buffer.unmap();`<br>`js/Time_Series.js:110` `buffer.destroy();`<br>`js/Time_Series.js:111` `return;`<br>`js/Time_Series.js:112` `}`<br>`js/Time_Series.js:166` ``headers += `Time${i+1}\tEta${i+1}\tP${i+1}\tQ${i+1}\t`;``.<br>확인한 원문: Tavakkol 2020 p.9; `js/Time_Series.js:98–118,166`; ebca435 커밋의 같은 파일 diff<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md)의 CP2-148. | C 문서 누락 | 시계열은 eta·P·Q를 별도 배열에 보관한다. 계측점이 있을 때 `if (timeSeriesDataEpoch != calc_constants.timeSeriesDataEpoch)`이면 readback 버퍼를 해제하고 반환한다. reset 전에 시작한 readback은 시계열 배열에 추가하지 않는다. [11-04 전수 검증 보완] |
| tavakkol2020:CP2-149 | 이미지·면 출력 선택과 주기 | Tavakkol 2020 p.1–2의 360도 영상 외에 이 옵션은 없다. | `js/constants_load_calc.js:358` `create_animation: 0, // create animated gif when = 1`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:359` `AnimGif_dt: 1.0, // time between animated gif frames`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:360` `JPEGstack_dt: 1.0,  // time between images in jpeg stack`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:361` `JPEGstack_frames: 10,  // total number of frames to save in jpg stack`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:362` `writesurfaces: 0,  //flag for writing 2D data to file`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:363` `write_eta: 1, // flag for writing eta surface data to file, write when = 1`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:364` `write_P: 0, // flag for writing x flux surface data to file, write when = 1`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:365` `write_Q: 0, // flag for writing y flux surface data to file, write when = 1`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:366` `write_u: 0, // flag for writing x velocity surface data to file, write when = 1`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:367` `write_v: 0, // flag for writing y velocity surface data to file, write when = 1`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:368` `write_turb: 0, // flag for writing eddy visc surface data to file, write when = 1`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:369` `dt_writesurface: 1.0, // incrememnt to write to file`<br>`js/constants_load_calc.js:370` `fileWritePause: 100, // time (ms) to pause during 2D surface write to not overload write buffer`<br>확인한 원문: Tavakkol 2020 p.1–2,9; `js/constants_load_calc.js:358–372`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md)의 CP2-149. | C 문서 누락 | GIF·JPEG 묶음·개별 면의 저장 플래그와 주기를 분리한다. 기본 면 변수 선택은 eta만 켜져 있다. 기본 `writesurfaces`는 0이므로 주기적 면 출력은 꺼져 있다. [11-04 전수 검증 보완] |
| tavakkol2020:CP2-150 | 중첩격자 경계 출력 | Tavakkol 2020 p.1–14에 대응 설명 없음. | `js/constants_load_calc.js:380–386` `nestedGridOutput_dt: 1.0, // requested nested boundary output interval`와 `nestedGridOutput_max_samples: 8192, // hard cap on stored output times`; `js/File_Writer.js:836` ``await blobCreationAndDownload(blob, `${prefix}_time_series_bc_${side.name}.txt`);``<br>확인한 원문: Tavakkol 2020 p.1–14; `js/constants_load_calc.js:374–392`; `js/File_Writer.js:730–755,810–836`; `js/File_Writer.js:690–706`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md)의 CP2-150. | C 문서 누락 | 사각형 경계의 eta/hu/hv 시계열을 저장한다. 시작·종료·최대 표본·복수 사각형 설정을 둔다. 양의 문턱을 설정하면 eta 절댓값이 문턱을 넘기 전의 선행 표본을 잘라 낸다. 이는 Base의 다섯 부이 출력과 다르다. |
| tavakkol2020:CP2-151 | 설정 병합과 파생값 | Tavakkol 2020 p.9는 CML 입력을 설명한다. JSON 병합·검사 규칙은 없다. | `js/constants_load_calc.js:425` `calc_constants = { ...calc_constants, ...loadedConfig };`; `js/constants_load_calc.js:438` `var configJson = JSON.parse(configContent);`; `js/constants_load_calc.js:489–502`는 Theta·Bcoef·역격자간격·delta·epsilon 파생식이다.<br>확인한 원문: Tavakkol 2020 p.9; `js/constants_load_calc.js:419–443,489–502`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md)의 CP2-151. | C 문서 누락 | 업로드/예제 값이 기본값을 덮는다. 이후 파생값을 계산한다. |
| tavakkol2020:CP2-152 | 수심 자료 검사와 가장자리 평탄화 | Tavakkol 2020 p.9는 CBF 자료·보간을 설명한다. | `js/File_Loader.js:50` `if (isNaN(parsedValue)) {`<br>`js/File_Loader.js:51` ``console.error(`Could not parse bathytopo value at [${x}, ${y}] in bathytopo file at ${filePath}`);``<br>`js/File_Loader.js:52` `return null;`<br>`js/File_Loader.js:53` `}`; `js/File_Loader.js:60–62` `for (let x = 0; x < 3; x++) {` / `bathy2D[x][y] = bathy2D[4][y];` / `}`; `js/File_Loader.js:65–67`는 오른쪽 네 열을 WIDTH−5 열로 채운다.<br>확인한 원문: Tavakkol 2020 p.9; `js/File_Loader.js:39–76`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md)의 CP2-152. | C 문서 누락 | 현재 loader는 숫자 파싱 실패를 거부한다. 왼쪽·아래쪽 세 줄과 오른쪽·위쪽 네 줄을 내부 값으로 평탄화한다. [11-04 전수 검증 보완] |
| tavakkol2020:CP2-159 | 계산·렌더 정밀도 분리 | Tavakkol 2020 p.5는 RenderTexture라고만 적는다. | `js/Create_Textures.js:5` `format: 'rgba32float',`; `js/Create_Textures.js:18` `format: 'rgba16float',`; `shaders/vertex3D.wgsl:78`은 전체 etaTexture를 직접 읽는다.<br>확인한 원문: Tavakkol 2020 p.5; `js/Create_Textures.js:2–18`; `js/main.js:451–452,559`; `shaders/vertex3D.wgsl:75–78`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md)의 CP2-159. | C 문서 누락 | 계산 상태의 float32와 렌더 cache의 float16을 구별한다. 3D 높이가 언제나 float16 cache에서 온다고 쓰지 않는다. |
| tavakkol2020:CP2-160 | MATLAB 출력 판독·정리 스크립트 | Tavakkol 2020 p.9는 출력의 상세 판독 절차를 적지 않는다. | `scripts/load_WebGPUCeleris_output.m:18` `bathytopo = fread(fid, [nx, ny], 'float32');`; `scripts/load_WebGPUCeleris_output.m:30` `eta = fread(fid, [nx, ny], 'float32');`; `scripts/load_WebGPUCeleris_output.m:73` `pcolor(x, y, eta');  % note the transpose '  - the surface data is [nx,ny]`; `scripts/clean_WebGPUCeleris_output.m:4–5` `! del *.bin` / `! del *.txt`<br>확인한 원문: Tavakkol 2020 p.9; `scripts/load_WebGPUCeleris_output.m:14–30,73`; `scripts/clean_WebGPUCeleris_output.m:4–5`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md)의 CP2-160. | C 문서 누락 | MATLAB은 `[nx,ny]`로 읽은 뒤 도식에서 전치한다. `nt=496`은 스크립트의 수동 수정값이다(`scripts/load_WebGPUCeleris_output.m:14`). 정리 스크립트는 Windows 삭제 명령이다. |
| tavakkol2020:CP2-162 | 설계 요소·마찰 변경 | Tavakkol 2020 p.1–14는 현재의 설계 요소별 매개변수를 적지 않는다. | `js/constants_load_calc.js:345–355`의 Friction·Coral·Oyster·Mangrove·Kelp·Grass·Scrub·RubbleMound·Dune·Berm·Seawall 기본값; `shaders/fragment.wgsl:638–645`의 component_index 분기<br>확인한 원문: Tavakkol 2020 p.1–14; `js/constants_load_calc.js:345–355`; `js/main.js:2514–2524,2567–2569`; `shaders/MouseClickChange.wgsl:234–239`; `shaders/fragment.wgsl:638–645`<br>최종 근거: [Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/Celeris-conclusion-verify-CP2.md)의 CP2-162. | C 문서 누락 | 현재 설계 요소의 기본 마찰값은 산호 0.1, 굴 0.035, 맹그로브 0.15, 해조 0.025, 잔디 0.03, 관목 0.075, 사석 0.04, 사구 0.03, berm 0.02, 방벽 0.02이다. 요소 추가 경로는 해당 값을 마찰맵에 반영한다. 이 값들을 2020 논문의 권고값으로 쓰지 않는다. |
